- #3931 (fixed; review: accept): The to-do reading took a date (and a name) out of the task text with no way to decline it, treated a bare weekday and a qualified one (next Friday) alike, and left a dangling 'next' when it cut the weekday out. The reading now reports each date and owner it took (Draft.DueReads, OwnerReads) and accepts the author's answers (Chatcmd004ParseTodoReviewed). Not verified or left: No browser. Journeywasm not run (only Draft JSON gained fields). Choices are keyed by task position, like the existing assign-N choices, so editing the text of an earlier task can shift them. Model 'Tidy with AI' keeps the earlier readings.
- #3933 (fixed; review: accept): The Saved panel and note editor never said that saved items, notes and reminders are personal. Added a persistent privacy line under the Saved header (#chatsave-privacy) and a private line in the note editor, wired to the note field with aria-describedby. en-US/de-DE/ar strings added to chatsave002Table. The existing row link and in-channel link remain the path back to the conversation; nothing is sent automatically. Not verified or left: Whole chatui package has 2 failures in other agents' areas (TestTodo_CHATBUG_036, TestTodo_CHATMAP_005_Browser). Wording not checked against admin policy (#847); no browser check. No separate Reply in conversation button added beyond existing open links.
- #3935 (partial; review: fixed in review): The message renderer only links http(s) addresses and in-app paths, but the composer gave no sign that a link destination such as example.com or the url placeholder would send as plain text. The Preview now shows a polite status note naming each link that will not render (placeholder, scheme-free web address with the explicit https:// form suggested, or unsupported scheme) with an Edit message button that leaves … Not verified or left: The warning appears only in Preview, not while typing; no one-click apply of the https:// suggestion; Send stays enabled; focus return after Edit message not exercised in a browser.
- #3939 (partial; review: accept): Draft writes are debounced 1.5 s for every change including an empty one, so a reload inside the window left the old text on the account. chatDraftWriter.Changed flushes immediately when the draft is empty (ordinary clear, /todo or /poll Cancel, Escape) and debounces otherwise; the browser DraftChanged callback now uses it. Not verified or left: The async write is a network call started right before reload and the browser may still cancel it; the write is not sent with keepalive or on pagehide, so an instant reload can still race. Room switches with an empty draft now trigger one extra drafts write.
- #3940 (fixed; review: accept): Date phrases were parsed with strconv.Atoi and ASCII digit checks, so Arabic-Indic and Persian digits never parsed and produced neither a date nor a warning. Digits U+0660-0669 and U+06F0-06F9 are normalised to 0-9 in the date resolver, the standalone-date check, the month-day reader and the unread-date check; the Arabic comma is a separator. Not verified or left: Other locales' normal numerals use Western digits and were not changed. Not driven in a browser.
- #3941 (fixed; review: accept): The open-result button was named from conversation, author and time only; the excerpt was a sibling div with no id and no aria-describedby. Each message result's excerpt div now has an id derived from the result's own target and kind, and its open button carries aria-describedby pointing at it, so the already bounded, highlighted excerpt is read with the button while the name keeps conversation, author and time. Not verified or left: No real screen reader run; the excerpt is the existing snippet of at most 320 characters and is not shortened further; file-result buttons (#3898) untouched.
- #3943 (partial; review: fixed in review): Who a draft's @mention names lives only in the mounted workspace's mention store, so a restored draft had the text but no outsider/reference record. New recoverDraftMentions runs from SettleOutsiders on render: once members are loaded it recognises a finished full-name @mention in the open draft; Not verified or left: Not driven in a browser. Gap: a saved mention whose person cannot be resolved (not in members or directory) is shown as plain text, with no 'unresolved' message, because no mention record survives a reload to distinguish it from typed text. Recovery needs the directory (SearchDirectory) loaded;
- #3944 (partial; review: accept): chatcmd003Settings never rendered a control for Poll.AddOptions, though the card, parser and Apply support author/members. Added a Who can add options radio group (Only me / Conversation members, default Only me) to the poll preview, wired through the existing set action. Not verified or left: Draft recovery: the choice lives in the preview's in-memory settings like the other settings, so a reload keeps it only if #3906 persists settings; I did not touch that. Author-only full-poll note is not shown (only members polls). Narrow layout not checked in a browser.
- #3950 (partial; review: fixed in review): A failed write of the drafts (backend away) was only retried by the next keystroke, so edits made during the outage stayed on screen but were never saved, with at most a generic save-your-sidebar notice; a reload then restored only the earlier saved text. Not verified or left: The wasm wiring (failure -> notice, retry, unload guard; success -> reset) was compiled and vetted but not run; no browser or backend-interruption run was possible.
- #3951 (fixed; review: accept): The parser kept the instant but dropped whether the author wrote a time, and the card and preview formatted only YYYY-MM-DD; the unread-date warning only ran after by/bis, so due/until/before gave no feedback. Tasks carry a due_timed flag (set for a full timestamp or in an hour / in one hour). Not verified or left: Time is shown with an ISO-like date and UTC offset in all locales (broader locale formatting is #3800). The 'due 03/04' warning and the explicit date control for editing/recovery were not added as a separate control; the existing composer text remains the way to edit.
- #3952 (partial; review: fixed in review): The assignee reader took the first @ mention and left every other mentioned person in the task text with no word to the author, so a second named person was silently not an assignee. A task that already has an assignee and still names another person with @ now records that person (with the owner's name) in the draft's Unresolved list. Not verified or left: Splitting into one task per person is not offered (only choosing the owner, which the issue allows as an alternative). Only @ mentions are detected, not plain names typed after the first.
- #3953 (fixed; review: fixed in review): Drafts are saved to the account through the sidebar blob with a revision. A revision-conflict rebase (and a later read) kept this tab's text and silently discarded the other tab's different text, with no indication and no way back. chatState now tracks the text the account is known to hold per conversation. Not verified or left: A whole journeywasm package run did not finish under the shared load (no result after 20+ minutes); only the subset above was run. No two-tab browser run. Reload before choosing leaves only the account's copy.
- #3961 (partial; review: fixed in review): A run reads thread posts with attached files as references only, and nothing told the person; the answer card gave no receipt of what was or was not read. Runs now count the files on the thread they read (ThreadPost.AttachmentCount, filled by both thread readers), carry the receipt through the model-work and delivery request, and append a typed trailing comment (chat.AttachmentReceiptMark) to the public and private … Not verified or left: Not exercised end to end: no agent run with a real attachment, (the delivery append is now covered by the review-added integration test, but BuildPersonaRunModelWork filling work.Attachments from a real thread is still only unit-tested through threadResult/personaThreadAttachmentReceipt).
- #3965 (partial; review: accept): The thread was held only in in-memory state and history.state; the address carried only #channel=..., so a full reload had nothing to restore it from. An open thread is now written to the address as #channel=&lt;room>&openthread=&lt;root id> (history push and replace, and returning from a page). Not verified or left: The wasm restore path (chatRestoreAddressedThread and the history writes) has no wasm test and was not run in a browser. A thread root older than the loaded window falls back to the unavailable notice rather than being fetched.
- #3969 (fixed; review: accept): The composer's Private answer switch only described who sees the answer and said nothing about the question staying in the channel; there was no way to move an unsent question into the person's own conversation with the agent. Not verified or left: No browser run: the click path (issue3969Click, domDraftValue, focus, SelectConversation with the draft appearing in the target composer) is only covered natively through issue3969Move/Cancel with recorded callbacks. Only the direct conversation already present in the conversation list is used;
- #3970 (fixed; review: accept): Chatcmd004ResolveDate built an ISO date's deadline as local midnight plus a fixed 23h59m, which crosses into the next day on a 23-hour DST day and ends an hour early on a 25-hour one. The ISO branch now builds 23:59 on the typed local date in the author's zone, matching the written-out-date path. Not verified or left: No browser run. internal/application/chatgate_consumers.go:61 uses the same fixed 23h59m addition for a different feature (gates); not changed, outside this issue.
- #3974 (fixed; review: fixed in review): The General agent description always said to add documents below, but the page draws the Add document picker only after the browser confirms the document hub; the server render never has one. The plain description no longer points at a control (new wording in en-US, de-DE, ar). The picker wording lives under a new key and is used only when the page draws the picker; Not verified or left: The browser swap of wording after the picker mounts, and why the demo omitted the picker, were not checked. No setup or recovery link is offered when documents are unavailable; the text just stays accurate.
- #3975 (partial; review: accept): The follow-up and the draft were handed over through session storage, and the browser-state policy (ValidateBrowserStorageWrite) refuses those keys, so every write was silently dropped and the composer returned blank after navigation. Ask a follow-up now quotes the request into the composer on the same page, keeps any unsent draft below the quote, focuses the composer and announces it in the polite status line. Not verified or left: Not run in a browser. The draft is in memory, so a full page reload (not the in-app round trip) still starts blank; deliberate, because storage policy forbids persisting typed text. Whether the round trip through Conversations is a software navigation (memory kept) or a full load was not confirmed.
- #3976 (fixed; review: fixed in review): A single loosely typed to-do line was always split at every comma (including thousands separators and commas inside quotes), and the only literal control ('Use what I typed') restores wording, not the split structure, because the split card is the stored original. The poll option splitter had the same blind spots. To-do: commas inside quotation marks or inside numbers ($5,000) no longer split; Not verified or left: No browser run. The region-comma poll rule is a heuristic (capitalised word, comma, two capital letters). 'Use what I typed' is unchanged by design; the new separate button restores the structure. Keep as one task also stops semicolon splitting for that line.
- #3981 (partial; review: accept): Close, Back and Forward change the address through the router but the Agents task region is drawn by the wasm island and was never redrawn from the address, so the detail and expanded row stayed. The focus-return hand-off used session storage, which the browser-state policy refuses. Not verified or left: The wasm wiring (popstate listener, delayed reconcile, focus return, filter after Close) is not exercised by any test and was not run in a browser. Full journeywasm package run not done; its test vet currently fails in another agent's file (issue3690_apply_test.go: undefined chatsearchControlBase).
- #3982 (fixed; review: accept): The count span carried aria-label 'Task count', which replaced its number in the tab's computed name. Each filter tab now has a localized accessible name with category and count and correct plurals (en-US 'Completed, 3 tasks', de-DE, ar with zero/one/two/few/many); the count span no longer has an aria-label. Counts are server rendered so they stay in sync. Updated two existing tests that asserted the old label. Not verified or left: No screen reader check; journeywasm not re-run for this change.
- #3983 (fixed; review: accept): hydrateAgentTaskPage prepended the task question to whatever the document title currently was whenever it did not already start with that question, so switching tasks stacked questions and closing never removed one. Not verified or left: Not run in a browser; full journeywasm suite not run. If the Chat tab-title sync also runs on the same page its base tracking is separate (attributes differ).
- #3984 (fixed; review: accept): The parser left an unresolved @name in the task and only added a generic 'a name fits no member' note; the preview did not name the token, say the task has no owner, or offer any way to choose an owner. The to-do parser now records each unresolved name (Draft.Unresolved: item and token). Not verified or left: No browser run. The picker lists only members already in this conversation (6 to 8 buttons), not a searchable directory, so a person outside the conversation cannot be picked and no distinction is made between an unknown name and a known non-member (deliberate, to avoid disclosing directory …
- #3987 (fixed; review: accept): render.go re-applies search-target to the row equal to Model.FocusMessageID on every render and nothing cleared that field on Jump to newest, while a stylesheet rule hid .jump-newest whenever any .message.search-target was mounted. Removed the rule that hid the shortcut while a target is mounted (highlight and focus behaviour untouched). Not verified or left: No browser check of the pin -> newest -> history -> newest cycle, narrow layouts or keyboard path. The full journeywasm package was not run; wasm go vet fails on another agent's test file (issue3768_list_history_test.go: chatNavigationPhone undefined).
- #3988 (partial; review: accept): A thread reply was rendered as a plain div.thread-message with no search-target class, no tabindex and no accessible name, so focus() did nothing and nothing marked the reply; the outline rule only matched .message.search-target. The reply whose id equals FocusMessageID now gets the search-target class, role=article, tabindex=0 and an accessible name 'Search result, reply by {author}, {time}' (en-US, de-DE, ar); Not verified or left: The workspace-search address (chatsearch002_workspace.go) still carries only the thread root and is pinned parent-only by chatsearch_lane18_test.go; not changed, so the outside-Chat entry does not yet carry the reply id.
- #3989 (partial; review: accept): FocusSearchMessage looked the row up once on the next frame; on a cross-channel open it runs before or during the destination timeline's first draw, and the room-switch rule then lands the list on its newest message (and images change heights later), leaving the hit thousands of px above the viewport. Not verified or left: No browser or wasm test run: the wasm loop in search_focus_js.go is not exercised by any executing test, and the existing wasm unit test for FocusSearchMessage was not run.
- #3991 (fixed; review: accept): The preview's settings were buttons with role=radio inside a radiogroup, but every button was a Tab stop and nothing handled arrow keys. Roving tabindex (the chosen option is the only Tab stop, or the first if none is chosen), ids on each radio, and an arrow/Home/End key handler on the preview slot that chooses the option it lands on and moves focus there. Left/Right turn round in RTL; moves wrap. Not verified or left: The DOM half (chatcmd003RadioTarget reading event.target, chatcmd003RadioFocus restoring focus after the re-render) compiles for wasm but was not run in a browser. Not tested: focus landing on the new option after the re-render.
- #3992 (partial; review: accept): localStore.forRoom reset the composer's Private answer switch to off on every conversation change and nothing kept it with the draft. The switch is now kept per conversation: leaving a conversation sets aside an 'on' choice and returning to that conversation restores it; other conversations start off, sent or turned-off drafts leave nothing. Not verified or left: Reload and Chat -> Agents -> Chat remounts lose the choice (localUI is in-memory and the draft store does not carry it); the restored draft then shows 'Everyone in #x will see the answer.' with the switch off.
- #3993 (partial; review: accept): Mention references (agent chip, colleague reference, nonmember warning) lived only in the mounted workspace's mentionBox, so leaving Chat dropped them while the draft text survived; and ReconcilePersonas/reconcileOutsiders ran every keystroke against all conversations' mentions, so typing in another room discarded the first room's mentions. Reconcile now only touches the open conversation's mentions. Not verified or left: No browser run. Reload (full page) restoration of the warning and chip is not implemented. Typed-but-unselected @Name indication not implemented. Real notification/invocation delivery and revocation against a live server not exercised.
- #3994 (fixed; review: accept): The work card decided 'Waiting on you' by case-insensitive equality of the viewer display name and the card's displayed approver/assignee, and for journeys treated every non-done stage as actionable. Previews now carry WaitsOnViewer, set by the browser resolver from stable subjects (approver/assignee id equals the signed-in subject) via new chatui.JourneyWaitsOnSubject and TaskWaitsOnSubject. Not verified or left: Role/delegation-based approval not modelled (journey detail exposes one approver subject, so delegates see an informational card). Authority is not rechecked on open here; the badge only labels and the journey/task services authorize on open. No served card with a real fixture checked.
- #3996 (fixed; review: accept): The thread pane always rendered the full root message (text, embeds, attachments) above the replies, so a tall root filled the panel. A tall root (over 280 characters, 4 or more lines, or any attachment) with replies opens clipped to a short faded summary under the author and date, with a Show full message / Show less toggle (aria-expanded, aria-controls, 44px target on touch, keyboard operable via the existing … Not verified or left: No browser check of the real clipping height, the fade, large images or phone width; the tall heuristic is length based, not measured. The toggle relies on re-render from the existing details-group action and was not clicked in a browser.
- #3997 (partial; review: fixed in review): The thread reply and the channel copy were two separately dispatched writes; the reply callback read the selected conversation inside its goroutine, and nothing remembered which half had committed, so a retry could duplicate the reply or skip the copy. Submit now captures conversation and parent together and hands them to a new ReplyInThreadIn callback. Not verified or left: Not run in a browser. Timeout-after-server-commit relies on idempotency-key reuse for identical text and was not exercised end to end; navigation-during-pending is covered at the dispatch level only (conversation and parent captured at submit).

## 2026-11-20 — Chat defect sweep: served-browser verification, first pass

The first-wave fixes were committed with rendered-output and state tests only. A server was rebuilt from that tree and each issue was then driven in Chromium with a live Playwright spec (one file per lane chunk, `tools/uxqual/browser/issue-*.live.spec.mjs`). Of 166 issues checked so far: 121 held as fixed, 30 did not hold, 3 had a remaining gap closed, 12 could not be checked in this demo (agents switched off, no journeys). 24 got a code fix in this pass; those fixes have unit tests but were made against the running build, so their specs stay red until the next rebuild. The specs are the durable record and are re-run after each rebuild.

- Select lists jumping to their last option (#3837, #3682): Neither app code nor hydration writes the wrong value. GoWebComponents v5.0.1 places a freshly mounted subtree a second time on the next commit: for a select that is one call select.append(option0..optionN) with the options the select already holds (commitWork placement branch on fibers that still carry effectTagPlacement with a dom, batched by BeginBatch/EndBatch into parent.append(...children) in internal/platform/jsdom/adapters.go appendDOMChildren). The browser carries out append(...nodes) by moving every node out first and inserting them together; Added a guard that keeps a select's chosen option chosen when its own options are appended to it again. It wraps append on HTMLSelectElement.prototype and HTMLOptGroupElement.prototype only: before the browser's append it notes the chosen option when the call moves options the select already owns (single-choice select, something chosen, no new option arriving chosen); after the append it chooses that option again if another one ended up chosen. Not verified or left: The live spec stays red on http://127.0.0.1:8080 until the coordinator rebuilds; rerun it then. The route-interception pass used a client built from the shared working tree, so it also contained other agents' client edits of that moment, including the data-chat-select-value attribute another agent added to the two search selects (which by itself re-applies those two);
- #57 (browser: pass): Dark colour scheme, /workspace/app/admin/chat-settings as Rafael at 1280x720 and 390x844: every .chatlangadmin panel (including those holding Location sharing and Voice messages, scrolled into view) has a dark effective background (luminance &lt; 0.35) and text at >= 4.5:1;
- #58 (browser: pass): Live spec as Rafael Torres opened /workspace/app/admin/chat-settings: POST /api/chat/locations/v1/settings returned 200 (statuses=[200]), the Location sharing heading is visible, the 'could not load or save' error text is absent, and form controls are present. Run result: ok. Left: The Try again retry path after a transient failure was not exercised.
- #59 (browser: pass): Dark colour scheme, 1280x720, #search=meeting and #search=calibration as Rafael: every one of the first (up to 6) result cards has author, date and snippet at contrast >= 4.5:1 and on a dark effective background (luminance &lt; 0.35). Left: Contrast of the search-hit highlight (mark) and hover surface not measured.
- #61 (browser: fail; fixed in this pass): Live run at 390 and 320 px (touch). Passing on the running build: results region is painted (>100px), exposed in the accessibility tree as region 'Results for meeting' with an 'Open result' button, hit-testing at the result centre lands on .chat-search-results, the rail is not aria-modal, main is … Fix: chatsearchModelOpened now also sets SidebarOpen=false, so opening a search result closes the phone Conversations drawer and shows the conversation. Unit test pins it. Left: After rebuild, re-run the spec: the tapped result must show its conversation (main visible, results gone) and Back to results must work.
- #71 (browser: not checkable): Not a browser issue (date-sensitive Go tests). Ran go test -count=1 ./internal/authn/enterprisegate/ (ok) and go test -count=1 -run 'TestIssue71|TestTodo_CHATUX_026' ./tools/uxqual/cmd/journeywasm/ (ok, 83.7s) on 2026-11-20. Left: Not run under another time zone.
- #82 (browser: pass): Live test opens Chat preferences from the gear and asserts the panel box is inside the viewport (x>=0, right&lt;=width) and, at >=700px, inside nav.chat-rail, at 800 ar, 800 en-US, 1440 ar, 1440 en-US, 390 ar, 320 en-US: 6 passed with --retries=0 (the 800px RTL case from the issue passes). Left: Dark theme and 390/320 in the other direction not run; issue itself says keep open until live retest passes, which this now does.
- #3398 (browser: pass): Live Playwright against http://127.0.0.1:8080 as Rafael Torres, route #search=budget, Refine search opened. For each of the seven controls (channel, person, before, after, on, kind, written) asserted it is visible, has an associated &lt;label> with non-empty text, all seven names are distinct, names … Left: Screen-reader speech and keyboard-only completion were not separately exercised beyond label association and the accessibility snapshot.

Outcome by issue: 17 complete, 1 already satisfied by the tree, 45 partial (a complete slice landed; the remainder is named per issue below). One agent implemented each issue test-first and a second reviewed it for wiring, authorization order, tenant scoping and copy, fixing what it found. These lanes ran concurrently in one checkout and share files (the renderer, the browser client, the chat store), so they are committed together. None of this wave was driven in a browser at this checkpoint, and the repository-wide gates (API and drift inventories, storage manifest, coverage floor) were not run on it yet.

## 2026-11-20 — Browser client size ceiling removed (owner decision); size attribution report (#3799, partial)

The second and third Chat feature waves grew `journey.wasm` from 60.8 MiB to 67.2 MiB (13.1 MiB compressed), past the 64 MiB and 13 MiB ceilings in `tools/uxqual/cmd/journeywasm/performance_budget.go`, so `scripts/build.sh hcmnext` and four tests refused the tree. The owner's instruction was to remove the ceiling rather than trim the client. `checkJourneyAssetBudgets` now only requires `journey.wasm` and its compressed form to exist and be non-empty; the `wasm_exec.js` ceilings stay. The tests that pinned the ceiling values and the headroom floors are changed to match: `client_bundle_headroom_test.go` is deleted (its only subject was headroom under the ceiling), `TestTodo_CHATUX_046_Integration` now requires the real build to succeed and records the sizes, `TestTodo_CHATUX_046` keeps its non-comparable-records leg, `TestTodo_CHATBUG_014` keeps the inlining and no-TLS-stack checks, and `TestTodo_WEB_236_PerformanceBudget` proves a large client passes while an over-size shim is still refused. This reverses the 2026-10-22 and 2026-11-04 entries above, which said to shrink the client instead of raising the ceiling; nothing now stops the client from growing, and cold-load time (UXBLIND-089) will get worse as it does.

#3799 landed its first slice only: a `-size-report` flag on the bundle builder that links a second copy with function names kept, attributes code bytes per package and group, and writes JSON or text outside the asset directory; `scripts/build.sh` takes `JOURNEY_SIZE_REPORT=1` and the CI build step uploads the report. Its ceiling and headroom fields were removed with the ceiling. Not done: moving anything out of the entry bundle.

Verified: `go test -count=1 -run 'WEB_236|CHATUX_046|CHATBUG_014$|Issue3799|TestBuildProducesBothHalvesOfTheBundle|TestTodo_WEB_031|TestIssue3798' ./tools/uxqual/cmd/journeywasm/` : ok (51s; includes a real client build). `go vet` native clean, gofmt clean. Not verified: the whole journeywasm package after this change; a served build.
